<file path="Printing/InvoicePrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class InvoicePrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColGreen = "#1E8449";
    private const string ColOrange = "#D35400";   // لون الخصم
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";   // لون الـ branding

    public static async Task PrintAsync(int invoiceId)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var invoice = await db.Invoices
            .Include(i => i.Customer)
            .Include(i => i.Lines)
            .FirstAsync(i => i.Id == invoiceId);

        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Invoice_{invoice.InvoiceNumber}.pdf");

        var doc = Document.Create(container =>
        {
            container.Page(page =>
            {
                page.Size(PageSizes.A4);
                page.Margin(2.2f, Unit.Centimetre);
                page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                    .FontFamily("Arial")
                    .FontSize(11)
                    .FontColor(ColDark)
                    .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                page.Content().Column(col =>
                {
                    // ─── Header ───────────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().Row(row =>
                    {
                        // LEFT visual: info box
                        row.ConstantItem(200)
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                            .Column(inner =>
                            {
                                inner.Item()
                                    .Text("فاتورة مبيعات")
                                    .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignCenter();

                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(8).Row(r =>
                                {
                                    r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                        .Text(invoice.InvoiceNumber).Bold();
                                    r.ConstantItem(8);
                                    r.ConstantItem(80).AlignLeft()
                                        .Text("رقم الفاتورة").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                                });
                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(4).Row(r =>
                                {
                                    r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                        .Text(invoice.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                                    r.ConstantItem(8);
                                    r.ConstantItem(80).AlignLeft()
                                        .Text("التاريخ").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                                });
                            });

                        row.ConstantItem(16);

                        // RIGHT visual: bank name + optional logo
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Row(r =>
                            {
                                if (logoBytes is not null)
                                {
                                    r.ConstantItem(52).Height(52)
                                        .Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                                    r.ConstantItem(10);
                                }
                                r.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
                                {
                                    inner.Item()
                                        .Text(org.BankName)
                                        .FontSize(logoBytes is null ? 26 : 20)
                                        .Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                                        inner.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                            .Text(org.SubTitle)
                                            .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Phone))
                                        inner.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                            .Text($"📞  {org.Phone}")
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Address))
                                        inner.Item()
                                            .Text(org.Address)
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                });
                            });
                        });
                    });

                    col.Item().PaddingVertical(14)
                        .LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);

                    // ─── Customer Info ────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Padding(14).Row(row =>
                    {
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text("رقم الهاتف")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                .Text(invoice.Customer.Phone ?? "—")
                                .FontSize(13).Bold().AlignLeft();
                        });

                        row.ConstantItem(16);
                        row.ConstantItem(1).Background(ColBorder);
                        row.ConstantItem(16);

                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text("بيانات العميل")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                .Text(invoice.Customer.Name)
                                .FontSize(13).Bold().AlignRight();
                        });
                    });

                    // ─── Payment & Notes ─────────────────────────────────
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(invoice.Notes) || invoice.PaymentType != PaymentType.Cash)
                    {
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(10).Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12).Row(row =>
                            {
                                row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                                {
                                    c.Item().Text("ملاحظات")
                                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                        .Text(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(invoice.Notes) ? "—" : invoice.Notes)
                                        .FontSize(11).AlignLeft();
                                });

                                row.ConstantItem(1).Background(ColBorder);
                                row.ConstantItem(16);

                                row.ConstantItem(160).Column(c =>
                                {
                                    c.Item().Text("طريقة الدفع")
                                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                        .Text(invoice.PaymentType switch
                                        {
                                            PaymentType.Cash => "نقدي",
                                            PaymentType.Credit => "آجل",
                                            PaymentType.Partial => $"جزئي — مدفوع: {invoice.PaidAmount:N2} ج.م",
                                            _ => invoice.PaymentType.ToString()
                                        })
                                        .FontSize(11).Bold().AlignRight();
                                });
                            });
                    }

                    // ─── Lines Table ──────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(18).Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
                    {
                        table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                        {
                            cols.ConstantColumn(100);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(2);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(2);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(4);
                            cols.ConstantColumn(36);
                        });

                        static IContainer TH(IContainer c) =>
                            c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                             .Background(ColPrimary)
                             .Padding(10).AlignCenter();

                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الإجمالي")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("سعر الوحدة")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الكمية")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الصنف / المنتج")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("#")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();

                        int i = 1;
                        foreach (var line in invoice.Lines)
                        {
                            var bg = i % 2 == 0 ? ColSurface : ColWhite;

                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text($"{line.Total:N2} ج.م").Bold().FontColor(ColGreen);
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text($"{line.UnitPrice:N2} ج.م");
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text(line.Quantity.ToString());
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignRight()
                                .Text(line.ProductName);
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text(i.ToString()).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                            i++;
                        }

                        static IContainer TD(IContainer c, string bg) =>
                            c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Background(bg).Padding(10);
                    });

                    // ─── Totals Box ───────────────────────────────────────
                    // يعرض: إجمالي الأصناف / الخصم (إن وجد) / صافي الفاتورة
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(2).Row(row =>
                    {
                        row.RelativeItem(); // spacer يدفع الجدول لليمين

                        row.ConstantItem(300)
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface)
                            .Column(totals =>
                            {
                                // ── صف: إجمالي الأصناف ───────────────────
                                totals.Item()
                                    .BorderBottom(invoice.DiscountAmount > 0 ? 0.5f : 0f)
                                    .BorderColor(ColBorder)
                                    .Padding(12)
                                    .Row(r =>
                                    {
                                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                            .Text($"{invoice.SubTotal:N2} ج.م")
                                            .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColDark);
                                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                                        r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                            .Text("إجمالي الأصناف")
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                    });

                                // ── صف: الخصم — يظهر فقط إذا كان > 0 ────
                                if (invoice.DiscountAmount > 0)
                                {
                                    totals.Item()
                                        .BorderBottom(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                                        .Padding(12)
                                        .Row(r =>
                                        {
                                            r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                                .Text(t =>
                                                {
                                                    t.Span("− ").FontColor(ColOrange).Bold();
                                                    t.Span($"{invoice.DiscountAmount:N2} ج.م")
                                                        .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColOrange);
                                                });
                                            r.ConstantItem(8);
                                            r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                                .Text("الخصم")
                                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                        });
                                }

                                // ── صف: صافي الفاتورة ────────────────────
                                totals.Item()
                                    .Background(ColPrimary)
                                    .Padding(14)
                                    .Row(r =>
                                    {
                                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                            .Text($"{invoice.TotalAmount:N2} ج.م")
                                            .FontSize(15).Bold().FontColor(ColWhite);
                                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                                        r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                            .Text("صافي الفاتورة")
                                            .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColWhite).AlignCenter();
                                    });
                            });
                    });

                    // ─── Thank-you + Branding ─────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(20).AlignCenter()
                        .Text("شكراً لتعاملكم معنا")
                        .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary);

                    col.Item().PaddingTop(8).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor("#EEEEEE");

                    col.Item().PaddingTop(6).AlignCenter()
                        .Text(t =>
                        {
                            t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand);
                            t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                            t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand);
                        });
                });
            });
        });

        doc.GeneratePdf(path);
        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
            new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/OrgInfoCache.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

/// <summary>
/// يُحمِّل إعدادات المؤسسة مرة واحدة ويخزّنها — استدعِ Invalidate()
/// بعد أي حفظ في OrganizationSettings لإعادة التحميل عند الطباعة التالية.
/// </summary>
public static class OrgInfoCache
{
    private static OrganizationSettings? _cached;

    public static async Task<OrganizationSettings> GetAsync()
    {
        if (_cached is not null) return _cached;
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        _cached = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync()
                  ?? new OrganizationSettings();
        return _cached;
    }

    public static void Invalidate() => _cached = null;
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/PaymentPrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class PaymentPrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";

    public static async Task PrintAsync(int voucherId)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var v = await db.ReceiptVouchers
            .Include(r => r.Safe)
            .FirstAsync(r => r.Id == voucherId);

        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Payment_{v.VoucherNumber}.pdf");
        var inWords = ArabicNumberHelper.ToWords(v.Amount);

        var doc = Document.Create(container =>
        {
            container.Page(page =>
            {
                page.Size(PageSizes.A4);
                page.Margin(1.4f, Unit.Centimetre);
                page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                    .FontFamily("Arial")
                    .FontSize(11)
                    .FontColor(ColDark)
                    .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                page.Content().Column(col =>
                {
                    string[] labels = { "نسخة العميل", "نسخة المحاسبة", "نسخة الأرشيف" };
                    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                    {
                        col.Item().Element(e => BuildCopy(e, v, org, logoBytes, inWords, labels[i]));
                        if (i < 2)
                            col.Item().PaddingVertical(6).AlignCenter()
                                .Text("- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBorder);
                    }
                });
            });
        });

        doc.GeneratePdf(path);
        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
            new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
    }

    private static void BuildCopy(
        IContainer container,
        ReceiptVoucher v,
        Models.OrganizationSettings org,
        byte[]? logoBytes,
        string words,
        string label)
    {
        container.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Padding(14).Column(col =>
        {
            // ── Header ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().Row(row =>
            {
                // LEFT: date + voucher number
                row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                {
                    c.Item().Text($"التاريخ: {DateTime.Now:d/M/yyyy}")
                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                        .Text(v.VoucherNumber)
                        .FontSize(9).Bold().FontColor(ColDark).AlignLeft();
                });

                row.ConstantItem(16);

                // CENTER: voucher title
                row.ConstantItem(160).Column(c =>
                {
                    c.Item()
                        .Background(ColPrimary).Padding(8)
                        .AlignCenter()
                        .Text("سند دفع نقدي")
                        .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold().FontSize(13);
                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                        .AlignCenter()
                        .Text(label)
                        .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                });

                row.ConstantItem(16);

                // RIGHT: bank name + logo
                row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                {
                    c.Item().Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
                        {
                            inner.Item()
                                .Text(org.BankName)
                                .FontSize(18).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignRight();
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                    .Text(org.SubTitle)
                                    .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                        });
                        if (logoBytes is not null)
                        {
                            r.ConstantItem(6);
                            r.ConstantItem(38).Height(38).Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                        }
                    });
                });
            });

            col.Item().PaddingVertical(10).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);

            // ── Details Table ────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
            {
                table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                {
                    cols.RelativeColumn(3);
                    cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                    cols.ConstantColumn(120);
                });

                void Row(string lbl, string val, bool highlight = false)
                {
                    var valueCell = table.Cell()
                        .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                        .Padding(10).AlignRight();

                    if (highlight)
                        valueCell.Text(val).Bold().FontSize(14).FontColor(ColPrimary);
                    else
                        valueCell.Text(val);

                    table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);

                    table.Cell()
                        .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                        .Background(ColSurface).Padding(10).AlignRight()
                        .Text(lbl).FontSize(9).SemiBold().FontColor(ColSecondary);
                }

                Row("رقم السند", v.VoucherNumber);
                Row("التاريخ", v.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                Row("الخزينة", v.Safe?.Name ?? "—");
                Row("صُرف إلى", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.PayeeName) ? "—" : v.PayeeName);
                Row("المبلغ", $"{v.Amount:N2} ج.م", highlight: true);
                Row("المبلغ كتابةً", words);
                Row("البيان", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.Notes) ? "—" : v.Notes);
            });

            col.Item().PaddingVertical(10);

            // ── Signatures ───────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
            {
                table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                {
                    cols.RelativeColumn();
                    cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                    cols.RelativeColumn();
                    cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                    cols.RelativeColumn();
                });

                void SigCell(string title)
                {
                    table.Cell()
                        .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                        .AlignCenter().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text(title)
                                .FontSize(9).SemiBold()
                                .FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(20)
                                .Text("_________________")
                                .FontColor(ColBorder).AlignCenter();
                        });
                }

                SigCell("المدير");
                table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                SigCell("المحاسب");
                table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                SigCell("أمين الخزينة");
            });

            // ── Branding ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().PaddingTop(6).AlignCenter()
                .Text(t =>
                {
                    t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                        .FontSize(7).FontColor(ColBrand);
                    t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                        .FontSize(7).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                    t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                        .FontSize(7).FontColor(ColBrand);
                });
        });
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/ReceiptPrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class ReceiptPrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";

    public static async Task PrintAsync(int voucherId)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var v = await db.ReceiptVouchers
            .Include(r => r.Customer)
            .Include(r => r.Safe)
            .FirstAsync(r => r.Id == voucherId);

        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Receipt_{v.VoucherNumber}.pdf");

        var doc = Document.Create(container =>
        {
            container.Page(page =>
            {
                page.Size(PageSizes.A5);
                page.Margin(1.8f, Unit.Centimetre);
                page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                    .FontFamily("Arial")
                    .FontSize(11)
                    .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                page.Content().Column(col =>
                {
                    // ── Header: شعار + اسم البنك ──────────────────────
                    col.Item().Row(row =>
                    {
                        if (logoBytes is not null)
                        {
                            row.ConstantItem(44).Height(44).Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                            row.ConstantItem(8);
                        }
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
                        {
                            inner.Item().AlignCenter()
                                .Text(org.BankName)
                                .FontSize(22).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                                inner.Item().AlignCenter()
                                    .Text(org.SubTitle)
                                    .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        });
                    });

                    col.Item().PaddingVertical(10)
                        .Background(ColDark).Padding(8).AlignCenter()
                        .Text("سند قبض").FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();

                    col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
                    {
                        table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                        {
                            cols.RelativeColumn();
                            cols.ConstantColumn(110);
                        });
                        AddRow(table, "رقم السند", v.VoucherNumber);
                        AddRow(table, "التاريخ", v.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                        AddRow(table, "الخزينة", v.Safe?.Name ?? "—");
                        AddRow(table, "العميل", v.Customer?.Name ?? "—");
                        AddRow(table, "المبلغ", $"{v.Amount:N2} ج.م", highlight: true);
                        AddRow(table, "البيان", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.Notes) ? "—" : v.Notes);
                    });

                    // ── Branding footer ────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(16).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor("#EEEEEE");
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(6).AlignCenter()
                        .Text(t =>
                        {
                            t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                                .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                            t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                                .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                            t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                                .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                        });
                });
            });
        });

        doc.GeneratePdf(path);
        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
            new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
    }

    private static void AddRow(TableDescriptor table, string label, string value, bool highlight = false)
    {
        table.Cell().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Padding(10).AlignRight()
            .Text(value).FontColor(highlight ? ColPrimary : ColDark);
        table.Cell().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Background(ColSurface)
            .Padding(10).AlignLeft().Text(label).SemiBold();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/SafeStatementPrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class SafeStatementPrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColGreen = "#1E8449";
    private const string ColRed = "#7B241C";
    private const string ColBlue = "#1A5276";
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";

    public static async Task PrintAsync(
        string safeName,
        DateTime from,
        DateTime to,
        decimal openingBalance,
        decimal closingBalance,
        decimal totalDebit,
        decimal totalCredit,
        List<SafeEntryRow> entries)
    {
        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        await Task.Run(() =>
        {
            var fileName = $"كشف_حساب_{safeName}_{from:yyyyMMdd}_{to:yyyyMMdd}.pdf";
            var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), fileName);

            var doc = Document.Create(container =>
            {
                container.Page(page =>
                {
                    page.Size(PageSizes.A4);
                    page.MarginTop(1.5f, Unit.Centimetre);
                    page.MarginBottom(1.5f, Unit.Centimetre);
                    page.MarginHorizontal(1.8f, Unit.Centimetre);

                    page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                        .FontFamily("Arial")
                        .FontSize(10)
                        .FontColor(ColDark)
                        .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                    page.Content().Column(col =>
                    {
                        col.Item().Element(BuildHeader(safeName, from, to, org, logoBytes));
                        col.Item().PaddingVertical(12).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);
                        col.Item().Element(BuildSummaryRow(openingBalance, totalDebit, totalCredit, closingBalance));
                        col.Item().PaddingVertical(14);
                        col.Item().Element(BuildTable(entries));
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(16).Element(BuildFooter());

                        // ── Branding ──────────────────────────────────
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(8).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor("#EEEEEE");
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(5).AlignCenter()
                            .Text(t =>
                            {
                                t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                                    .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                                t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                                    .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                                t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                                    .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                            });
                    });

                    page.Footer().AlignCenter().Text(t =>
                    {
                        t.Span("صفحة ").FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.CurrentPageNumber().FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.Span(" من ").FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.TotalPages().FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.Span($"   |   طُبع في: {DateTime.Now:d/M/yyyy  HH:mm}")
                            .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBorder);
                    });
                });
            });

            doc.GeneratePdf(path);
            System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
                new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
        });
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  رأس الصفحة
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildHeader(
        string safeName, DateTime from, DateTime to,
        Models.OrganizationSettings org, byte[]? logoBytes)
        => c => c.Row(row =>
        {
            row.ConstantItem(230)
                .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                .Column(inner =>
                {
                    inner.Item().Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight().Text(safeName).Bold();
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignLeft().Text("الخزينة").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                    inner.Item().PaddingTop(4).Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight().Text(from.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignLeft().Text("من").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                    inner.Item().PaddingTop(4).Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight().Text(to.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignLeft().Text("إلى").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                });

            row.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
            {
                inner.Item().Row(r =>
                {
                    r.RelativeItem().Column(bc =>
                    {
                        bc.Item()
                            .Text(org.BankName)
                            .FontSize(22).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                            bc.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                .Text("كشف حساب خزينة — " + org.SubTitle)
                                .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        else
                            bc.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                .Text("كشف حساب خزينة")
                                .FontSize(11).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Phone))
                            bc.Item().Text($"📞  {org.Phone}")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                    if (logoBytes is not null)
                    {
                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                        r.ConstantItem(52).Height(52).Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                    }
                });
            });
        });

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  ملخص الأرصدة
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildSummaryRow(
        decimal openingBalance, decimal totalDebit, decimal totalCredit, decimal closingBalance)
        => c => c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
        {
            table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
            {
                cols.RelativeColumn();
                cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                cols.RelativeColumn();
                cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                cols.RelativeColumn();
                cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                cols.RelativeColumn();
            });

            void Card(string label, decimal value, string color)
            {
                table.Cell()
                    .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                    .AlignCenter().Column(cc =>
                    {
                        cc.Item().Text(label)
                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                        cc.Item().PaddingTop(5)
                            .Text($"{value:N2} ج.م")
                            .FontSize(13).Bold().FontColor(color).AlignCenter();
                    });
            }

            void Divider() => table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);

            Card("الرصيد الختامي", closingBalance, closingBalance >= 0 ? ColGreen : ColPrimary);
            Divider();
            Card("إجمالي الصادر", totalCredit, ColRed);
            Divider();
            Card("إجمالي الوارد", totalDebit, ColGreen);
            Divider();
            Card("الرصيد الافتتاحي", openingBalance, ColBlue);
        });

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  جدول الحركات
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildTable(List<SafeEntryRow> entries)
        => c => c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
        {
            table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
            {
                cols.ConstantColumn(105);
                cols.ConstantColumn(95);
                cols.ConstantColumn(95);
                cols.ConstantColumn(62);
                cols.RelativeColumn(3);
                cols.ConstantColumn(78);
            });

            void TH(string text)
            {
                table.Cell()
                    .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                    .Background(ColDark).Padding(9)
                    .AlignCenter()
                    .Text(t => t.Span(text).FontColor(ColWhite).Bold().FontSize(10));
            }

            TH("الرصيد");
            TH("صادر  ▼");
            TH("وارد  ▲");
            TH("النوع");
            TH("البيان / الوصف");
            TH("التاريخ");

            int i = 0;
            foreach (var e in entries)
            {
                var bg = i % 2 == 0 ? ColWhite : ColSurface;

                void TD(string txt, string? color = null, bool bold = false, bool center = false)
                {
                    var cell = table.Cell()
                        .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                        .Background(bg).Padding(7);

                    IContainer aligned = center ? cell.AlignCenter() : (IContainer)cell;
                    aligned.Text(t =>
                    {
                        var span = t.Span(txt).FontSize(9.5f);
                        if (color != null) span = span.FontColor(color);
                        if (bold) span = span.Bold();
                    });
                }

                var balColor = e.Balance >= 0 ? ColGreen : ColPrimary;
                TD($"{e.Balance:N2}", color: balColor, bold: true);

                if (e.Credit > 0)
                    TD($"{e.Credit:N2}", color: ColRed, bold: true);
                else
                    TD("—", color: ColBorder, center: true);

                if (e.Debit > 0)
                    TD($"{e.Debit:N2}", color: ColGreen, bold: true);
                else
                    TD("—", color: ColBorder, center: true);

                TD(e.RefTypeDisplay, center: true, color: ColSecondary);
                TD(e.Description);
                TD(e.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"), center: true, color: ColSecondary);

                i++;
            }

            // صف الإجمالي
            void TF(string txt, string bg2 = "#1D1D1F", string color = "#FFFFFF")
            {
                table.Cell()
                    .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                    .Background(bg2).Padding(9)
                    .Text(t => t.Span(txt).FontColor(color).FontSize(10).Bold());
            }

            TF(entries.Count > 0 ? $"{entries.Last().Balance:N2} ج.م" : "0.00 ج.م",
               entries.Count > 0 && entries.Last().Balance >= 0 ? ColBlue : ColRed,
               ColWhite);
            TF($"{entries.Sum(e => e.Credit):N2} ج.م", ColRed, ColWhite);
            TF($"{entries.Sum(e => e.Debit):N2} ج.م", ColGreen, ColWhite);
            TF("", ColDark, ColWhite);
            TF("الإجمالي", ColDark, ColWhite);
            TF("", ColDark, ColWhite);
        });

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  ذيل الصفحة — التوقيعات
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildFooter()
        => c => c.Column(col =>
        {
            col.Item().LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);
            col.Item().PaddingTop(14)
                .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                .Table(table =>
                {
                    table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                    {
                        cols.RelativeColumn();
                        cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                        cols.RelativeColumn();
                        cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                        cols.RelativeColumn();
                    });

                    void SigCell(string title)
                    {
                        table.Cell()
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(14)
                            .AlignCenter().Column(c2 =>
                            {
                                c2.Item().Text(title)
                                    .FontSize(10).SemiBold()
                                    .FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                c2.Item().PaddingTop(22)
                                    .Text("_________________")
                                    .FontColor(ColBorder).AlignCenter();
                            });
                    }

                    SigCell("المدير");
                    table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                    SigCell("المحاسب");
                    table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                    SigCell("أمين الخزينة");
                });
        });
}

// ── Extension helpers ──────────────────────────────────────
internal static class QuestExtensions
{
    internal static IContainer When(
        this IContainer c,
        bool condition,
        Func<IContainer, IContainer> apply)
        => condition ? apply(c) : c;

    internal static TextSpanDescriptor When(
        this TextSpanDescriptor t,
        bool condition,
        Func<TextSpanDescriptor, TextSpanDescriptor> apply)
        => condition ? apply(t) : t;
}

using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
