// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CUSTOMERS
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class CustomersViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Customers { get; } = [];

    [ObservableProperty] private Customer? _selected;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _filterText = string.Empty;

    // Form fields
    [ObservableProperty] private string _name = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private CustomerType _type = CustomerType.Hospital;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _phone = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _creditLimit;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isEditing;

    public IEnumerable<CustomerType> CustomerTypes { get; } = Enum.GetValues<CustomerType>();

    public CustomersViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var list = await db.Customers.OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
        Customers.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Customers.Add);
    }

    partial void OnSelectedChanged(Customer? value)
    {
        if (value is null) return;
        Name = value.Name;
        Type = value.Type;
        Phone = value.Phone;
        CreditLimit = value.CreditLimit;
        IsEditing = true;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NewCustomer()
    {
        Selected = null;
        Name = string.Empty;
        Phone = string.Empty;
        CreditLimit = 0;
        Type = CustomerType.Hospital;
        IsEditing = true;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Name)) { ShowError("اسم العميل مطلوب"); return; }
        using var db = new AppDbContext();

        if (Selected is null)
        {
            db.Customers.Add(new Customer { Name = Name, Type = Type, Phone = Phone, CreditLimit = CreditLimit });
        }
        else
        {
            var c = await db.Customers.FindAsync(Selected.Id);
            c!.Name = Name; c.Type = Type; c.Phone = Phone; c.CreditLimit = CreditLimit;
        }

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        IsEditing = false;
        await LoadAsync();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SearchAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var q = FilterText.Trim();
        var list = await db.Customers
            .Where(c => string.IsNullOrEmpty(q) || c.Name.Contains(q) || c.Phone.Contains(q))
            .OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
        Customers.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Customers.Add);
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  BLOOD INVENTORY
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class BloodInventoryViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<BloodBag> Bags { get; } = [];

    // Form
    [ObservableProperty] private string _bagNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private BloodGroup _bloodGroup = BloodGroup.APositive;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _costPrice;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _salePrice;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _expiryDate = DateTime.Today.AddDays(35);
    [ObservableProperty] private BloodBagStatus _filterStatus = BloodBagStatus.Available;

    public IEnumerable<BloodGroup> BloodGroups { get; } = Enum.GetValues<BloodGroup>();
    public IEnumerable<BloodBagStatus> StatusOptions { get; } = Enum.GetValues<BloodBagStatus>();

    public BloodInventoryViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    partial void OnFilterStatusChanged(BloodBagStatus value) { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var list = await db.BloodBags
                               .Where(b => b.Status == FilterStatus)
                               .OrderBy(b => b.ExpiryDate)
                               .ToListAsync();
        Bags.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Bags.Add);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task AddBagAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(BagNumber)) { ShowError("رقم الكيس مطلوب"); return; }
        if (SalePrice <= 0) { ShowError("سعر البيع مطلوب"); return; }

        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        if (await db.BloodBags.AnyAsync(b => b.BagNumber == BagNumber))
        { ShowError("رقم الكيس مستخدم بالفعل"); return; }

        db.BloodBags.Add(new BloodBag
        {
            BagNumber = BagNumber,
            BloodGroup = BloodGroup,
            CostPrice = CostPrice,
            SalePrice = SalePrice,
            ExpiryDate = ExpiryDate
        });
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        BagNumber = string.Empty;
        await LoadAsync();
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  INVOICE LIST
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class InvoiceListViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<Invoice> Invoices { get; } = [];

    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _from = DateTime.Today.AddMonths(-1);
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _to = DateTime.Today;

    public InvoiceListViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var list = await db.Invoices
                               .Include(i => i.Customer)
                               .Where(i => i.Date.Date >= From && i.Date.Date <= To)
                               .OrderByDescending(i => i.Date)
                               .ToListAsync();
        Invoices.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Invoices.Add);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintInvoiceAsync(Invoice invoice)
    {
        await InvoicePrinter.PrintAsync(invoice.Id);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task CancelInvoiceAsync(Invoice invoice)
    {
        if (!Confirm($"إلغاء الفاتورة {invoice.InvoiceNumber}؟")) return;
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var inv = await db.Invoices.Include(i => i.Lines).FirstAsync(i => i.Id == invoice.Id);
        inv.IsCancelled = true;
        inv.CancelReason = "إلغاء يدوي";

        foreach (var line in inv.Lines)
        {
            var bag = await db.BloodBags.FindAsync(line.BloodBagId);
            if (bag is not null) bag.Status = BloodBagStatus.Available;
        }

        switch (inv.PaymentType)
        {
            case PaymentType.Credit:
                var cCredit = await db.Customers.FindAsync(inv.CustomerId);
                cCredit!.Balance -= inv.TotalAmount;
                break;

            case PaymentType.Partial:
                var cPartial = await db.Customers.FindAsync(inv.CustomerId);
                cPartial!.Balance -= inv.RemainingAmount;
                if (inv.SafeId.HasValue)
                {
                    var safeP = await db.Safes.FindAsync(inv.SafeId);
                    safeP!.Balance -= inv.PaidAmount;
                }
                break;
        }

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        await LoadAsync();
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  DEBT REPORT
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class DebtReportViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Debtors { get; } = [];
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalDebt;

    public DebtReportViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var list = await db.Customers
                               .Where(c => c.Balance > 0)
                               .OrderByDescending(c => c.Balance)
                               .ToListAsync();
        Debtors.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Debtors.Add);
        TotalDebt = list.Sum(c => c.Balance);
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ORGANIZATION SETTINGS  (الإعدادات — جديد)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class OrgSettingsViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ─── بيانات البنك ─────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _bankName = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _subTitle = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _address = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _phone = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string? _logoPath;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasLogo;

    // ─── الرمز السري ──────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasPin;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _pinStatusMessage = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasPinError;

    private string _currentPinHash = string.Empty;

    public OrgSettingsViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync()
                  ?? new OrganizationSettings();

        BankName = org.BankName;
        SubTitle = org.SubTitle;
        Address = org.Address;
        Phone = org.Phone;
        LogoPath = org.LogoPath;
        HasLogo = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && System.IO.File.Exists(org.LogoPath);

        _currentPinHash = org.OwnerPinHash;
        HasPin = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentPinHash);
    }

    // ─── حفظ بيانات البنك ─────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveOrgInfoAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(BankName))
        {
            ShowError("اسم بنك الدم مطلوب");
            return;
        }

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            if (org is null)
            {
                org = new OrganizationSettings { Id = 1 };
                db.OrgSettings.Add(org);
            }

            org.BankName = BankName.Trim();
            org.SubTitle = SubTitle.Trim();
            org.Address = Address.Trim();
            org.Phone = Phone.Trim();
            org.LogoPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(LogoPath) ? null : LogoPath.Trim();

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            OrgInfoCache.Invalidate();   // إعادة تحميل عند الطباعة التالية
            StatusMessage = "✓  تم حفظ بيانات البنك بنجاح";
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ─── شعار البنك ───────────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void BrowseLogo()
    {
        var dlg = new OpenFileDialog
        {
            Title = "اختر صورة شعار البنك",
            Filter = "صور (*.png;*.jpg;*.jpeg;*.bmp)|*.png;*.jpg;*.jpeg;*.bmp"
        };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog() != true) return;

        LogoPath = dlg.FileName;
        HasLogo = true;
        StatusMessage = string.Empty;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void RemoveLogo()
    {
        LogoPath = null;
        HasLogo = false;
    }

    // ─── الرمز السري (يُستدعى من Code-behind لأن PasswordBox لا يدعم Binding) ──
    public void ChangePinFromView(string currentPin, string newPin)
    {
        PinStatusMessage = string.Empty;
        HasPinError = false;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newPin))
        {
            PinStatusMessage = "أدخل الرمز الجديد أولاً";
            HasPinError = true;
            return;
        }

        // إذا كان هناك رمز سابق — تحقق منه أولاً
        if (HasPin && !PinHelper.Verify(currentPin, _currentPinHash))
        {
            PinStatusMessage = "الرمز الحالي غير صحيح";
            HasPinError = true;
            return;
        }

        _ = SaveNewPinAsync(newPin);
    }

    public void RemovePinFromView(string currentPin)
    {
        PinStatusMessage = string.Empty;
        HasPinError = false;

        if (!PinHelper.Verify(currentPin, _currentPinHash))
        {
            PinStatusMessage = "الرمز الحالي غير صحيح";
            HasPinError = true;
            return;
        }

        _ = SaveNewPinAsync(string.Empty);   // رمز فارغ = بدون حماية
    }

    private async Task SaveNewPinAsync(string newPin)
    {
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            var newHash = PinHelper.Hash(newPin);

            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            if (org is null) { org = new OrganizationSettings { Id = 1 }; db.OrgSettings.Add(org); }

            org.OwnerPinHash = newHash;
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            _currentPinHash = newHash;
            HasPin = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(newHash);
            PinStatusMessage = HasPin ? "✓  تم حفظ الرمز السري بنجاح" : "✓  تم إزالة الرمز السري";
            HasPinError = false;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            PinStatusMessage = ex.Message;
            HasPinError = true;
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }




    //.............................
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  CHART DATA CLASSES  (لا تغيير عليهم)
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    /// <summary>نقطة بيانات يومية لرسم مبيعات وإيرادات آخر 7 أيام</summary>
    public class DailySalesPoint
    {
        public string Label { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string DateLabel { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public decimal Sales { get; set; }
        public decimal Revenue { get; set; }
        public bool IsToday { get; set; }

        public double SalesBarHeight { get; set; }
        public double SalesSpacerHeight { get; set; }
        public double RevenueBarHeight { get; set; }
        public double RevenueSpacerHeight { get; set; }
    }

    /// <summary>فصيلة دم واحدة لرسم التوزيع الأفقي</summary>
    public class BloodGroupItem
    {
        public string GroupName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public int Count { get; set; }
        public double BarWidth { get; set; }
        public System.Windows.Media.SolidColorBrush Brush { get; set; }
            = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.DarkRed;
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  DASHBOARD VIEW MODEL  — استبدل الكلاس الموجود بهذا
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    public partial class DashboardViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        // ─── إحصائيات اليوم ──────────────────────────────────────
        /// <summary>إجمالي قيمة الفواتير المُصدرة اليوم</summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _todaySales;

        /// <summary>الفلوس الداخلة فعلاً اليوم — نقدي + جزئي + سندات قبض</summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _todayRevenue;

        [ObservableProperty] private int _todayInvoicesCount;

        /// <summary>فواتير نقدي + جزئي اليوم (تم تحصيلها)</summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private int _todayCashInvoices;

        /// <summary>فواتير آجل اليوم (لم تُحصَّل بعد)</summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private int _todayCreditInvoices;

        /// <summary>نسبة الإيرادات من المبيعات اليوم (0–100+)</summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _collectionRateToday;

        // ─── إحصائيات الشهر ──────────────────────────────────────
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _monthSales;
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _monthRevenue;
        [ObservableProperty] private int _monthInvoicesCount;

        /// <summary>متوسط المبيعات اليومي هذا الشهر</summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _averageDailySales;

        /// <summary>نسبة التحصيل الشهرية (0–100+)</summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _collectionRateMonth;

        // ─── مؤشرات مستمرة ───────────────────────────────────────
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalDebt;
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _safeBalance;
        [ObservableProperty] private int _availableBags;
        [ObservableProperty] private int _expiringBags;
        [ObservableProperty] private string _topDebtorName = "—";
        [ObservableProperty] private decimal _topDebtorBalance;

        // ─── بيانات الرسوم البيانية ───────────────────────────────
        public ObservableCollection<DailySalesPoint> WeeklySales { get; } = [];
        public ObservableCollection<BloodGroupItem> BloodGroupStats { get; } = [];

        // ─── القفل المالي ─────────────────────────────────────────
        [ObservableProperty] private bool _isFinancialUnlocked = true;
        private string _ownerPinHash = string.Empty;

        public string LockIcon => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "🔓" : "🔒";
        public string LockButtonText => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "إخفاء البيانات" : "عرض البيانات المالية";

        // ─── خصائص محسوبة للرسوم ─────────────────────────────────
        /// <summary>عرض شريط التحصيل اليومي بالبكسل — max = 180</summary>
        public double CollectionRateTodayWidth
            => (double)Math.Min(CollectionRateToday, 100) / 100.0 * 180.0;

        /// <summary>عرض شريط التحصيل الشهري بالبكسل — max = 180</summary>
        public double CollectionRateMonthWidth
            => (double)Math.Min(CollectionRateMonth, 100) / 100.0 * 180.0;

        /// <summary>هل توجد أكياس ستنتهي صلاحيتها خلال أسبوع؟</summary>
        public bool HasExpiringBags => ExpiringBags > 0;

        // ─── Partial change handlers ─────────────────────────────
        partial void OnIsFinancialUnlockedChanged(bool value)
        {
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockIcon));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockButtonText));
        }

        partial void OnCollectionRateTodayChanged(decimal value)
            => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CollectionRateTodayWidth));

        partial void OnCollectionRateMonthChanged(decimal value)
            => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CollectionRateMonthWidth));

        partial void OnExpiringBagsChanged(int value)
            => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasExpiringBags));

        // ─── Constructor ─────────────────────────────────────────
        public DashboardViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

        // ─── Load ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        private async Task LoadAsync()
        {
            IsBusy = true;
            try
            {
                using var db = new AppDbContext();
                var today = DateTime.Today;
                var nextWeek = today.AddDays(7);
                var monthStart = new DateTime(today.Year, today.Month, 1);

                // ══ إحصائيات اليوم ════════════════════════════════

                TodaySales = await db.Invoices
                    .Where(i => i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled)
                    .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.TotalAmount) ?? 0;

                // إيرادات = PaidAmount من نقدي/جزئي + سندات قبض
                TodayRevenue = await db.Invoices
                    .Where(i => i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled
                             && i.PaymentType != PaymentType.Credit)
                    .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.PaidAmount) ?? 0;
                TodayRevenue += await db.ReceiptVouchers
                    .Where(v => v.Date.Date == today && v.Type == VoucherType.Receipt)
                    .SumAsync(v => (decimal?)v.Amount) ?? 0;

                TodayInvoicesCount = await db.Invoices
                    .CountAsync(i => i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled);

                // تصنيف الفواتير: نقدي/جزئي (محصّل) vs آجل (لم يُحصَّل)
                TodayCashInvoices = await db.Invoices.CountAsync(i =>
                    i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled &&
                    (i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash || i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial));

                TodayCreditInvoices = await db.Invoices.CountAsync(i =>
                    i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled &&
                    i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Credit);

                CollectionRateToday = TodaySales > 0
                    ? Math.Round(TodayRevenue / TodaySales * 100, 1)
                    : 0;

                // ══ إحصائيات الشهر ════════════════════════════════

                MonthSales = await db.Invoices
                    .Where(i => i.Date >= monthStart && !i.IsCancelled)
                    .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.TotalAmount) ?? 0;

                MonthRevenue = await db.Invoices
                    .Where(i => i.Date >= monthStart && !i.IsCancelled
                             && i.PaymentType != PaymentType.Credit)
                    .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.PaidAmount) ?? 0;
                MonthRevenue += await db.ReceiptVouchers
                    .Where(v => v.Date >= monthStart && v.Type == VoucherType.Receipt)
                    .SumAsync(v => (decimal?)v.Amount) ?? 0;

                MonthInvoicesCount = await db.Invoices
                    .CountAsync(i => i.Date >= monthStart && !i.IsCancelled);

                CollectionRateMonth = MonthSales > 0
                    ? Math.Round(MonthRevenue / MonthSales * 100, 1)
                    : 0;

                // متوسط يومي = مجموع مبيعات الشهر ÷ عدد الأيام المنقضية
                int daysElapsed = Math.Max(today.Day, 1);
                AverageDailySales = Math.Round(MonthSales / daysElapsed, 0);

                // ══ مؤشرات مستمرة ════════════════════════════════

                TotalDebt = await db.Customers.SumAsync(c => (decimal?)c.Balance) ?? 0;
                SafeBalance = await db.Safes.SumAsync(s => (decimal?)s.Balance) ?? 0;
                AvailableBags = await db.BloodBags.CountAsync(b => b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available);
                ExpiringBags = await db.BloodBags.CountAsync(b =>
                    b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available && b.ExpiryDate <= nextWeek);

                var topDebtor = await db.Customers
                    .Where(c => c.Balance > 0)
                    .OrderByDescending(c => c.Balance)
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync();
                TopDebtorName = topDebtor?.Name ?? "لا توجد ديون";
                TopDebtorBalance = topDebtor?.Balance ?? 0;

                // ══ رسم مبيعات آخر 7 أيام ════════════════════════

                var weekStart = today.AddDays(-6);
                var rawSales = await db.Invoices
                    .Where(i => i.Date.Date >= weekStart && i.Date.Date <= today && !i.IsCancelled)
                    .GroupBy(i => i.Date.Date)
                    .Select(g => new
                    {
                        Date = g.Key,
                        Sales = g.Sum(i => i.TotalAmount),
                        Revenue = g.Sum(i => i.PaidAmount)
                    })
                    .ToListAsync();

                const double MaxBarH = 110.0;
                const double MinBarH = 4.0;

                var maxSalesVal = rawSales.Any() ? (double)rawSales.Max(d => d.Sales) : 1.0;
                if (maxSalesVal == 0) maxSalesVal = 1;

                var weekPoints = new List<DailySalesPoint>();
                for (int d = 6; d >= 0; d--)
                {
                    var date = today.AddDays(-d);
                    var data = rawSales.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Date == date);
                    var sVal = (double)(data?.Sales ?? 0);
                    var rVal = (double)(data?.Revenue ?? 0);

                    double sH = sVal > 0 ? Math.Max(MinBarH, sVal / maxSalesVal * MaxBarH) : 0;
                    double rH = rVal > 0 ? Math.Max(MinBarH, rVal / maxSalesVal * MaxBarH) : 0;

                    weekPoints.Add(new DailySalesPoint
                    {
                        Label = GetArabicDayName(date),
                        DateLabel = date.ToString("d/M"),
                        Sales = data?.Sales ?? 0,
                        Revenue = data?.Revenue ?? 0,
                        SalesBarHeight = sH,
                        SalesSpacerHeight = MaxBarH - sH,
                        RevenueBarHeight = rH,
                        RevenueSpacerHeight = MaxBarH - rH,
                        IsToday = date == today
                    });
                }
                WeeklySales.Clear();
                weekPoints.ForEach(WeeklySales.Add);

                // ══ توزيع فصائل الدم ══════════════════════════════

                var bagGroups = await db.BloodBags
                    .Where(b => b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available)
                    .GroupBy(b => b.BloodGroup)
                    .Select(g => new { Group = g.Key, Count = g.Count() })
                    .ToListAsync();

                var groupColors = new Dictionary<BloodGroup, string>
            {
                { BloodGroup.APositive,  "#C0392B" }, { BloodGroup.ANegative,  "#922B21" },
                { BloodGroup.BPositive,  "#2471A3" }, { BloodGroup.BNegative,  "#1A5276" },
                { BloodGroup.ABPositive, "#1E8449" }, { BloodGroup.ABNegative, "#196F3D" },
                { BloodGroup.OPositive,  "#D35400" }, { BloodGroup.ONegative,  "#BA4A00" },
            };

                int maxCount = bagGroups.Any() ? bagGroups.Max(g => g.Count) : 1;
                if (maxCount == 0) maxCount = 1;

                BloodGroupStats.Clear();
                foreach (var bg in bagGroups.OrderByDescending(g => g.Count))
                {
                    var hex = groupColors.GetValueOrDefault(bg.Group, "#666666");
                    var brushColor = (System.Windows.Media.Color)
                        System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(hex);

                    BloodGroupStats.Add(new BloodGroupItem
                    {
                        GroupName = bg.Group switch
                        {
                            BloodGroup.APositive => "A+",
                            BloodGroup.ANegative => "A-",
                            BloodGroup.BPositive => "B+",
                            BloodGroup.BNegative => "B-",
                            BloodGroup.ABPositive => "AB+",
                            BloodGroup.ABNegative => "AB-",
                            BloodGroup.OPositive => "O+",
                            BloodGroup.ONegative => "O-",
                            _ => bg.Group.ToString()
                        },
                        Count = bg.Count,
                        BarWidth = (double)bg.Count / maxCount * 200.0,
                        Brush = new System.Windows.Media.SolidColorBrush(brushColor)
                    });
                }

                // ══ إعدادات القفل ═════════════════════════════════
                var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
                _ownerPinHash = org?.OwnerPinHash ?? string.Empty;
                IsFinancialUnlocked = string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash);
            }
            finally { IsBusy = false; }
        }

        private static string GetArabicDayName(DateTime date) => date.DayOfWeek switch
        {
            DayOfWeek.Saturday => "سبت",
            DayOfWeek.Sunday => "أحد",
            DayOfWeek.Monday => "اثن",
            DayOfWeek.Tuesday => "ثلاث",
            DayOfWeek.Wednesday => "أرب",
            DayOfWeek.Thursday => "خمس",
            DayOfWeek.Friday => "جمعة",
            _ => ""
        };

        [RelayCommand]
        private void ToggleFinancialLock()
        {
            if (IsFinancialUnlocked)
            {
                IsFinancialUnlocked = false;
                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash))
            {
                IsFinancialUnlocked = true;
                return;
            }

            var enteredPin = Views.PinDialog.Show(
                System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow);
            if (enteredPin is null) return;

            if (PinHelper.Verify(enteredPin, _ownerPinHash))
                IsFinancialUnlocked = true;
            else
                ShowError("الرمز السري غير صحيح");
        }
    }





}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/Base/ViewModelBase.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;

public abstract partial class ViewModelBase : ObservableObject
{
    [ObservableProperty]
    private bool _isBusy;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _statusMessage = string.Empty;

    protected static void ShowError(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "خطأ", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);

    protected static void ShowSuccess(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "تم", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);

    protected static bool Confirm(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "تأكيد", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Question) == MessageBoxResult.Yes;
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/InvoiceViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class InvoiceViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ─── Header ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _invoiceNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(IsSafeVisible))]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(IsPartialVisible))]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(RemainingAfterInitial))]
    private PaymentType _paymentType = PaymentType.Cash;

    [ObservableProperty] private Customer? _selectedCustomer;
    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(RemainingAfterInitial))]
    private decimal _initialPayment;

    // ─── Quick Add Customer ───────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isQuickAddVisible;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _newCustomerName = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private CustomerType _newCustomerType = CustomerType.Hospital;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _newCustomerPhone = string.Empty;

    public IEnumerable<CustomerType> CustomerTypes { get; } = Enum.GetValues<CustomerType>();

    // ─── Manual Line Entry ────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private string _newLineName = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private int _newLineQty = 1;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private decimal _newLineUnitPrice;

    public decimal NewLineTotal => NewLineQty * NewLineUnitPrice;

    // ─── Barcode Scan (secondary) ─────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _scanBagNumber = string.Empty;

    // ─── Lines ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    public ObservableCollection<InvoiceLineItem> Lines { get; } = [];

    // ─── Totals ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _discountAmount;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _subTotal;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalAmount;

    public bool IsSafeVisible => PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash || PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial;
    public bool IsPartialVisible => PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial;
    public decimal RemainingAfterInitial => Math.Max(0, TotalAmount - InitialPayment);

    // ─── Lookup lists ─────────────────────────────────────────
    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Customers { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<BloodBag> AvailableBags { get; } = [];

    public IEnumerable<PaymentType> PaymentTypes { get; } = Enum.GetValues<PaymentType>();

    /// <summary>أنواع منتجات الدم الشائعة — قابلة للتحرير الحر في ComboBox</summary>
    public IReadOnlyList<string> BloodProductTypes { get; } =
    [
        "دم كامل",
        "بلازما طازجة مجمدة",
        "صفائح دموية",
        "كريات حمراء مضغوطة",
        "بلازما ناضجة",
        "ترسيب قري",
        "مستحضرات بلازما",
    ];

    public InvoiceViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();

            var customers = await db.Customers.Where(c => c.IsActive).OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
            var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();
            var bags = await db.BloodBags
                .Where(b => b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available && b.ExpiryDate > DateTime.Today)
                .OrderBy(b => b.ExpiryDate)
                .ToListAsync();

            Customers.Clear(); customers.ForEach(Customers.Add);
            Safes.Clear(); safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);
            AvailableBags.Clear(); bags.ForEach(AvailableBags.Add);

            InvoiceNumber = await NumberGenerator.NextInvoiceNumberAsync(db);
            SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain);
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ─── Quick Add Customer ───────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void ToggleQuickAdd()
    {
        IsQuickAddVisible = !IsQuickAddVisible;
        NewCustomerName = string.Empty;
        NewCustomerPhone = string.Empty;
        NewCustomerType = CustomerType.Hospital;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task QuickSaveCustomerAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NewCustomerName))
        {
            ShowError("أدخل اسم العميل");
            return;
        }

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();

            var customer = new Customer
            {
                Name = NewCustomerName.Trim(),
                Type = NewCustomerType,
                Phone = NewCustomerPhone.Trim(),
                IsActive = true
            };

            db.Customers.Add(customer);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            Customers.Add(customer);
            SelectedCustomer = customer;
            IsQuickAddVisible = false;
            NewCustomerName = string.Empty;
            NewCustomerPhone = string.Empty;
            ShowSuccess($"تمت إضافة العميل «{customer.Name}» ✓");
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ─── إضافة صنف يدوي ──────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void AddManualLine()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NewLineName))
        {
            ShowError("أدخل اسم الصنف"); return;
        }
        if (NewLineQty <= 0)
        {
            ShowError("الكمية يجب أن تكون أكبر من صفر"); return;
        }
        if (NewLineUnitPrice <= 0)
        {
            ShowError("أدخل سعر الوحدة"); return;
        }

        var line = new InvoiceLineItem
        {
            BloodBagId = null,
            IsManual = true,
            ProductName = NewLineName.Trim(),
            UnitPrice = NewLineUnitPrice,
            Quantity = NewLineQty
        };
        line.PropertyChanged += (_, _) => RecalculateTotals();
        Lines.Add(line);
        RecalculateTotals();

        // إعادة تعيين حقول الإدخال
        NewLineName = string.Empty;
        NewLineQty = 1;
        NewLineUnitPrice = 0;
    }

    // ─── إضافة كيس بالنقر ────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void AddLine(BloodBag bag)
    {
        if (Lines.Any(l => l.BloodBagId == bag.Id))
        {
            ShowError("هذا الكيس مضاف بالفعل"); return;
        }

        var line = new InvoiceLineItem
        {
            BloodBagId = bag.Id,
            IsManual = false,
            ProductName = $"{bag.BagNumber} — {bag.BloodGroup.ToArabic()}",
            UnitPrice = bag.SalePrice,
            Quantity = 1
        };
        line.PropertyChanged += (_, _) => RecalculateTotals();
        Lines.Add(line);
        RecalculateTotals();
    }

    // ─── إضافة كيس بالباركود ─────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void AddLineByBarcode()
    {
        var barcode = ScanBagNumber.Trim();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(barcode)) return;

        var bag = AvailableBags.FirstOrDefault(b =>
            string.Equals(b.BagNumber.Trim(), barcode, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

        if (bag is null) ShowError($"لا يوجد كيس متاح بهذا الباركود:\n{barcode}");
        else AddLine(bag);

        ScanBagNumber = string.Empty;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void RemoveLine(InvoiceLineItem line)
    {
        Lines.Remove(line);
        RecalculateTotals();
    }

    partial void OnDiscountAmountChanged(decimal value) => RecalculateTotals();

    private void RecalculateTotals()
    {
        SubTotal = Lines.Sum(l => l.Total);
        TotalAmount = Math.Max(0, SubTotal - DiscountAmount);
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RemainingAfterInitial));
    }

    // ─── Save ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (!Validate()) return;

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var invoice = new Invoice
            {
                InvoiceNumber = InvoiceNumber,
                Date = Date,
                PaymentType = PaymentType,
                CustomerId = SelectedCustomer!.Id,
                SafeId = IsSafeVisible ? SelectedSafe!.Id : null,
                SubTotal = SubTotal,
                DiscountAmount = DiscountAmount,
                TotalAmount = TotalAmount,
                PaidAmount = PaymentType switch
                {
                    PaymentType.Cash => TotalAmount,
                    PaymentType.Partial => InitialPayment,
                    _ => 0
                }
            };

            db.Invoices.Add(invoice);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            foreach (var line in Lines)
            {
                db.InvoiceLines.Add(new InvoiceLine
                {
                    InvoiceId = invoice.Id,
                    BloodBagId = line.BloodBagId,            // null للأصناف اليدوية
                    ProductName = line.ProductName,
                    UnitPrice = line.UnitPrice,
                    Quantity = line.Quantity,
                    Total = line.Total
                });

                // تحديث حالة الكيس فقط إذا كان مرتبطاً
                if (line.BloodBagId.HasValue)
                {
                    var bag = await db.BloodBags.FindAsync(line.BloodBagId.Value);
                    if (bag is not null) bag.Status = BloodBagStatus.Sold;
                }
            }

            var customer = await db.Customers.FindAsync(SelectedCustomer!.Id);

            switch (PaymentType)
            {
                case PaymentType.Cash:
                    var safeCash = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
                    safeCash!.Balance += TotalAmount;
                    break;

                case PaymentType.Credit:
                    customer!.Balance += TotalAmount;
                    break;

                case PaymentType.Partial:
                    customer!.Balance += (TotalAmount - InitialPayment);
                    var safePartial = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
                    safePartial!.Balance += InitialPayment;
                    break;
            }

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            invoice.Customer = customer!;

            if (PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial)
                JournalService.PostPartialInvoice(db, invoice, InitialPayment, AccountCodes.Cash);
            else
                JournalService.PostInvoice(db, invoice,
                    PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash ? AccountCodes.Cash : AccountCodes.AccountsRec);

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            await tx.CommitAsync();

            ShowSuccess($"تم حفظ الفاتورة {InvoiceNumber} بنجاح ✓");
            await ResetFormAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            // ابحث عن السبب العميق للخطأ
            var message = ex.InnerException?.InnerException?.Message
                          ?? ex.InnerException?.Message
                          ?? ex.Message;

            ShowError($"حدث خطأ أثناء الحفظ:\n{message}");
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    private bool Validate()
    {
        if (SelectedCustomer is null) { ShowError("اختر العميل"); return false; }
        if (!Lines.Any()) { ShowError("أضف صنفاً واحداً على الأقل"); return false; }

        // ─── التحقق من الخصم ──────────────────────────────────
        if (DiscountAmount < 0)
        {
            ShowError("الخصم لا يمكن أن يكون قيمة سالبة"); return false;
        }
        if (DiscountAmount > SubTotal)
        {
            ShowError("الخصم لا يمكن أن يتجاوز إجمالي الفاتورة"); return false;
        }

        if (PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash && SelectedSafe is null)
        { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
        if (PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial)
        {
            if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
            if (InitialPayment <= 0) { ShowError("أدخل المبلغ المدفوع الآن"); return false; }
            if (InitialPayment >= TotalAmount)
            { ShowError("المبلغ المدفوع يجب أن يكون أقل من الإجمالي\nللدفع الكامل اختر «نقدي»"); return false; }
        }
        return true;
    }

    private async Task ResetFormAsync()
    {
        Lines.Clear();
        SelectedCustomer = null;
        DiscountAmount = 0;
        InitialPayment = 0;
        ScanBagNumber = string.Empty;
        IsQuickAddVisible = false;
        NewLineName = string.Empty;
        NewLineQty = 1;
        NewLineUnitPrice = 0;
        await LoadAsync();
    }
}

// ─── Line item DTO ────────────────────────────────────────────
public partial class InvoiceLineItem : ObservableObject
{
    /// <summary>null → صنف يدوي</summary>
    public int? BloodBagId { get; set; }
    public bool IsManual { get; set; }
    public string ProductName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(Total))]
    private decimal _unitPrice;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(Total))]
    private int _quantity = 1;

    public decimal Total => UnitPrice * Quantity;
}

// ─── Extension ────────────────────────────────────────────────
public static class BloodGroupExtensions
{
    public static string ToArabic(this BloodGroup g) => g switch
    {
        BloodGroup.APositive => "A+",
        BloodGroup.ANegative => "A-",
        BloodGroup.BPositive => "B+",
        BloodGroup.BNegative => "B-",
        BloodGroup.ABPositive => "AB+",
        BloodGroup.ABNegative => "AB-",
        BloodGroup.OPositive => "O+",
        BloodGroup.ONegative => "O-",
        _ => g.ToString()
    };
}

<file path="ViewModels/PaymentVoucherViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.Printing;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;


namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class PaymentVoucherViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _voucherNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;

    /// <summary>اسم المستلم (موظف / مورّد / جهة)</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private string _payeeName = string.Empty;

    /// <summary>البيان — وصف سبب الصرف</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private string? _notes;

    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _amount;
    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;

    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];

    /// <summary>رقم السند المحفوظ — يُستخدم لطباعة السند بعد الحفظ</summary>
    private int? _savedVoucherId;

    public bool CanPrint => _savedVoucherId.HasValue;

    public PaymentVoucherViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();
        Safes.Clear(); safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);
        VoucherNumber = await NumberGenerator.NextPaymentVoucherNumberAsync(db);
        SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain);
        _savedVoucherId = null;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanPrint));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (!Validate()) return;

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var voucher = new ReceiptVoucher
            {
                VoucherNumber = VoucherNumber,
                Type = VoucherType.Payment,
                Date = Date,
                SafeId = SelectedSafe!.Id,
                PayeeName = PayeeName.Trim(),
                Amount = Amount,
                Notes = Notes
            };

            db.ReceiptVouchers.Add(voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // خصم المبلغ من الخزينة (المال يخرج)
            var safe = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
            safe!.Balance -= Amount;

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // قيد المحاسبة: مصاريف مدين — خزينة دائن
            JournalService.PostPayment(db, voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            await tx.CommitAsync();

            _savedVoucherId = voucher.Id;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanPrint));
            ShowSuccess($"تم حفظ سند الدفع {VoucherNumber} ✓");
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintAsync()
    {
        if (!_savedVoucherId.HasValue) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try { await PaymentPrinter.PrintAsync(_savedVoucherId.Value); }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task NewVoucherAsync()
    {
        PayeeName = string.Empty;
        Notes = null;
        Amount = 0;
        await LoadAsync();
    }

    private bool Validate()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(PayeeName)) { ShowError("أدخل اسم المستلم"); return false; }
        if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
        if (Amount <= 0) { ShowError("المبلغ يجب أن يكون أكبر من صفر"); return false; }

        // تحقق من الرصيد الكافي
        if (SelectedSafe.Balance < Amount)
        {
            ShowError($"رصيد الخزينة غير كافٍ\nالرصيد الحالي: {SelectedSafe.Balance:N2} ج.م\nالمطلوب صرفه: {Amount:N2} ج.م");
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

<file path="ViewModels/ReceiptVoucherViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class ReceiptVoucherViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _voucherNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _amount;
    [ObservableProperty] private string? _notes;

    [ObservableProperty] private Customer? _selectedCustomer;
    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;
    [ObservableProperty] private Invoice? _selectedInvoice;

    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Customers        { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<Safe>     Safes            { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<Invoice>  CustomerInvoices { get; } = [];

    public ReceiptVoucherViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db  = new AppDbContext();
        var customers = await db.Customers.Where(c => c.IsActive && c.Balance > 0)
                                          .OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
        var safes     = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();

        Customers.Clear(); customers.ForEach(Customers.Add);
        Safes.Clear();     safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);

        VoucherNumber = await NumberGenerator.NextVoucherNumberAsync(db);
        SelectedSafe  = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain);
    }

    partial void OnSelectedCustomerChanged(Customer? value)
    {
        _ = LoadCustomerInvoicesAsync(value);
    }

    private async Task LoadCustomerInvoicesAsync(Customer? customer)
    {
        CustomerInvoices.Clear();
        if (customer is null) return;

        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var invoices = await db.Invoices
            .Where(i => i.CustomerId == customer.Id
                     && (i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Credit || i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial)
                     && !i.IsCancelled
                     && i.PaidAmount < i.TotalAmount)   // لا تزال عليه دفعات
            .OrderByDescending(i => i.Date)
            .ToListAsync();

        invoices.ForEach(CustomerInvoices.Add);
        Amount = customer.Balance;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (!Validate()) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db  = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var voucher = new ReceiptVoucher
            {
                VoucherNumber    = VoucherNumber,
                Date             = Date,
                SafeId           = SelectedSafe!.Id,
                CustomerId       = SelectedCustomer!.Id,
                RelatedInvoiceId = SelectedInvoice?.Id,
                Amount           = Amount,
                Notes            = Notes
            };

            db.ReceiptVouchers.Add(voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ─── Update balances ─────────────────────────────
            var customer = await db.Customers.FindAsync(SelectedCustomer!.Id);
            customer!.Balance -= Amount;

            var safe = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
            safe!.Balance += Amount;

            if (SelectedInvoice is not null)
            {
                var invoice = await db.Invoices.FindAsync(SelectedInvoice.Id);
                invoice!.PaidAmount += Amount;
            }

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ─── Journal ─────────────────────────────────────
            voucher.Customer = customer;
            JournalService.PostReceipt(db, voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            await tx.CommitAsync();
            ShowSuccess($"تم حفظ سند القبض {VoucherNumber} ✓");
            await LoadAsync();
            SelectedCustomer = null;
            Amount = 0;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    private bool Validate()
    {
        if (SelectedCustomer is null) { ShowError("اختر العميل"); return false; }
        if (SelectedSafe is null)     { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
        if (Amount <= 0)              { ShowError("المبلغ يجب أن يكون أكبر من صفر"); return false; }
        return true;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/SafeReportViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.Printing;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class SafeReportViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  القفل المالي
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isFinancialUnlocked = true;
    private string _ownerPinHash = string.Empty;

    public string LockIcon => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "🔓" : "🔒";
    public string LockButtonText => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "إخفاء البيانات" : "عرض البيانات المالية";

    partial void OnIsFinancialUnlockedChanged(bool value)
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockIcon));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockButtonText));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ToggleFinancialLock()
    {
        if (IsFinancialUnlocked) { IsFinancialUnlocked = false; return; }

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash)) { IsFinancialUnlocked = true; return; }

        var enteredPin = Views.PinDialog.Show(System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow);
        if (enteredPin is null) return;

        if (PinHelper.Verify(enteredPin, _ownerPinHash))
            IsFinancialUnlocked = true;
        else
            ShowError("الرمز السري غير صحيح");
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  بيانات الخزينة
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<SafeEntryRow> Entries { get; } = [];

    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _from = DateTime.Today.AddMonths(-1);
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _to = DateTime.Today;

    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _openingBalance;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _closingBalance;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalDebit;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalCredit;

    public SafeReportViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();
        Safes.Clear();
        safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);
        SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain) ?? safes.FirstOrDefault();

        var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
        _ownerPinHash = org?.OwnerPinHash ?? string.Empty;

        IsFinancialUnlocked = string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash);
    }

    partial void OnSelectedSafeChanged(Safe? value)
    {
        if (value is not null) _ = LoadStatementAsync();
    }

    partial void OnFromChanged(DateTime value) { if (SelectedSafe is not null) _ = LoadStatementAsync(); }
    partial void OnToChanged(DateTime value) { if (SelectedSafe is not null) _ = LoadStatementAsync(); }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task LoadStatementAsync()
    {
        if (SelectedSafe is null) return;

        using var db = new AppDbContext();

        // ← الإصلاح: نطابق الـ AccountCode الفعلي اللي بيحفظه JournalService
        var safeCode = SelectedSafe.Type == SafeType.Cash
            ? AccountCodes.Cash   // "1001"
            : AccountCodes.Bank;  // "1002"

        var beforeDebit = await db.JournalEntries
            .Where(j => j.Date < From && j.AccountCode == safeCode)
            .SumAsync(j => (decimal?)j.DebitAmount) ?? 0;

        var beforeCredit = await db.JournalEntries
            .Where(j => j.Date < From && j.AccountCode == safeCode)
            .SumAsync(j => (decimal?)j.CreditAmount) ?? 0;

        OpeningBalance = beforeDebit - beforeCredit;

        var entries = await db.JournalEntries
            .Where(j => j.Date >= From && j.Date <= To && j.AccountCode == safeCode)
            .OrderBy(j => j.Date)
            .ToListAsync();

        Entries.Clear();
        var runningBalance = OpeningBalance;

        foreach (var e in entries)
        {
            runningBalance += e.DebitAmount - e.CreditAmount;
            Entries.Add(new SafeEntryRow
            {
                Date = e.Date,
                RefType = e.RefType,        // ← مهم للطباعة الفردية
                RefId = e.RefId,          // ← مهم للطباعة الفردية
                RefTypeDisplay = e.RefType switch
                {
                    "Invoice" => "فاتورة",
                    "Receipt" => "قبض",
                    "Payment" => "صرف",
                    _ => e.RefType
                },
                Description = e.AccountName,
                Debit = e.DebitAmount,
                Credit = e.CreditAmount,
                Balance = runningBalance
            });
        }

        TotalDebit = entries.Sum(e => e.DebitAmount);
        TotalCredit = entries.Sum(e => e.CreditAmount);
        ClosingBalance = runningBalance;
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  طباعة الكشف كله
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintAsync()
    {
        if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة أولاً"); return; }
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            await SafeStatementPrinter.PrintAsync(
                SelectedSafe.Name,
                From, To,
                OpeningBalance, ClosingBalance,
                TotalDebit, TotalCredit,
                Entries.ToList());
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  طباعة حركة واحدة بعينها
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintEntryAsync(SafeEntryRow entry)
    {
        if (entry is null) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            switch (entry.RefType)
            {
                case "Invoice":
                    await InvoicePrinter.PrintAsync(entry.RefId);
                    break;
                case "Receipt":
                    await ReceiptPrinter.PrintAsync(entry.RefId);
                    break;
                case "Payment":
                    await PaymentPrinter.PrintAsync(entry.RefId);
                    break;
                default:
                    ShowError($"لا توجد طباعة متاحة لهذا النوع: {entry.RefTypeDisplay}");
                    break;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError($"خطأ في الطباعة:\n{ex.Message}"); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  صف كشف الحساب
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public class SafeEntryRow
{
    public DateTime Date { get; set; }
    public string RefType { get; set; } = string.Empty;   // ← Invoice / Receipt / Payment
    public int RefId { get; set; }                   // ← Id الوثيقة الأصلية
    public string RefTypeDisplay { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Description { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public decimal Debit { get; set; }
    public decimal Credit { get; set; }
    public decimal Balance { get; set; }
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/TrialViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class TrialViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _activationKey = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _machineId;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasError;

    // FIX: اسم مختلف عن StatusMessage في ViewModelBase لتفادي التعارض
    [ObservableProperty] private string _trialStatus = string.Empty;

    // FIX: نص زر النسخ — بيتغير لحظة الضغط ويرجع تاني بعد ثانيتين
    [ObservableProperty] private string _copyButtonText = "📋 نسخ";

    public int DaysRemaining { get; } = TrialManager.DaysRemaining();
    public bool IsExpired { get; } = TrialManager.DaysRemaining() == 0;

    public event Action? ActivationSucceeded;

    private readonly DispatcherTimer _copyResetTimer;

    public TrialViewModel()
    {
        _machineId = TrialManager.GetMachineId();

        _copyResetTimer = new DispatcherTimer
        {
            Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2)
        };
        _copyResetTimer.Tick += (_, _) =>
        {
            CopyButtonText = "📋 نسخ";
            TrialStatus = string.Empty;
            _copyResetTimer.Stop();
        };
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void Activate()
    {
        HasError = false;
        TrialStatus = string.Empty;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ActivationKey))
        {
            HasError = true;
            TrialStatus = "أدخل كود التفعيل";
            return;
        }

        if (TrialManager.Activate(ActivationKey))
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                "✅ تم تفعيل البرنامج بنجاح!\nشكراً لثقتكم في شريان.",
                "تم التفعيل",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Information);

            ActivationSucceeded?.Invoke();
        }
        else
        {
            HasError = true;
            TrialStatus = "كود التفعيل غير صحيح. تأكد من الكود وحاول مجدداً.";
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CopyMachineId()
    {
        Clipboard.SetText(MachineId);

        // FIX: تغيير فوري لزر النسخ + رسالة نجاح خضراء
        CopyButtonText = "✅ تم";
        TrialStatus = "✓ تم نسخ معرّف الجهاز";
        HasError = false;

        _copyResetTimer.Stop();
        _copyResetTimer.Start();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ContinueTrial()
    {
        ActivationSucceeded?.Invoke();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/BloodInventoryView.xaml.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class BloodInventoryView : UserControl
{
    public BloodInventoryView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        // ??? ????? ??????? ???????? ??? ????? ?????? ????????
        // ????? ??? ???? ???????? ??? ??? ????? ???? ??????
        // ???????? ????? ?????? ?????? ?????? ??? ?? ???.
        Loaded += (_, _) =>
        {
            TxtBagNumber.Focus();
            ActivateScanBorder();
        };

        TxtBagNumber.GotFocus += (_, _) => ActivateScanBorder();
        TxtBagNumber.LostFocus += (_, _) => DeactivateScanBorder();
    }

    // ??? ??? ?????? ???????? ? ????? ????? ?????????????????
    private void ScanIconBorder_Click(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseButtonEventArgs e)
    {
        TxtBagNumber.Focus();
        TxtBagNumber.SelectAll();
    }

    // ??? ????? ??? ?????? ??? ??????? ??????????????????????
    private void ActivateScanBorder()
    {
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B)); // PrimaryBrush
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(2);
        ScanIconBorder.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
    }

    private void DeactivateScanBorder()
    {
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xE5, 0xE8, 0xEC));
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.5);
        ScanIconBorder.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x1A, 0x27, 0x44));
    }
}
</file>

                    </DataGridTextColumn>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="اسم العميل"
                                       Binding="{Binding Name}" Width="*"/>

<file path="Views/InvoiceView.xaml.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class InvoiceView : UserControl
{
    public InvoiceView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        // ??? ????? ??? ????? ??? ????? ???? ???????? ????????
        Loaded += (_, _) =>
        {
            TxtScanInvoice.Focus();
            ActivateInvoiceScanBorder();
        };

        TxtScanInvoice.GotFocus += (_, _) => ActivateInvoiceScanBorder();
        TxtScanInvoice.LostFocus += (_, _) => DeactivateInvoiceScanBorder();
    }

    // ??? ??? ?????? ???????? ????????????????????????????????
    private void InvoiceScanIcon_Click(object sender,
        System.Windows.Input.MouseButtonEventArgs e)
    {
        TxtScanInvoice.Focus();
        TxtScanInvoice.SelectAll();
    }

    private void ActivateInvoiceScanBorder()
    {
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(2);
        InvoiceScanIcon.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
    }

    private void DeactivateInvoiceScanBorder()
    {
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xE5, 0xE8, 0xEC));
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.5);
        InvoiceScanIcon.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x1A, 0x27, 0x44));
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/OrganizationSettingsView.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class OrganizationSettingsView : UserControl
{
    public OrganizationSettingsView() => InitializeComponent();

    // PasswordBox لا يدعم Binding مباشر لأسباب أمنية — نمرر القيمة عبر Code-behind
    private void OnChangePinClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (DataContext is not OrgSettingsViewModel vm) return;
        vm.ChangePinFromView(CurrentPinBox.Password, NewPinBox.Password);
        CurrentPinBox.Clear();
        NewPinBox.Clear();
    }

    private void OnRemovePinClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (DataContext is not OrgSettingsViewModel vm) return;
        vm.RemovePinFromView(CurrentPinBox.Password);
        CurrentPinBox.Clear();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/PinDialog.xaml.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class PinDialog : Window
{
    public string EnteredPin { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public PinDialog()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        OkBtn.Click += (_, _) => Confirm();
        CancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => { DialogResult = false; Close(); };
        PinBox.KeyDown += (_, e) => { if (e.Key == Key.Return) Confirm(); };
        Loaded += (_, _) => PinBox.Focus();

        // السحب من الشريط الفارغ
        MouseLeftButtonDown += (_, e) => { if (e.ButtonState == MouseButtonState.Pressed) DragMove(); };
    }

    private void Confirm()
    {
        ErrorText.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;   // Hidden لا Collapsed للحفاظ على المساحة
        EnteredPin = PinBox.Password;
        DialogResult = true;
        Close();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// عرض الحوار وإرجاع الرمز المُدخَل، أو null عند الإلغاء.
    /// </summary>
    public static string? Show(Window? owner = null)
    {
        var dlg = new PinDialog();
        if (owner is not null) dlg.Owner = owner;
        return dlg.ShowDialog() == true ? dlg.EnteredPin : null;
    }

    /// <summary>يُظهر رسالة الخطأ ويمسح الحقل (للاستخدام الخارجي)</summary>
    public void ShowError()
    {
        ErrorText.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
        PinBox.Clear();
        PinBox.Focus();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/TrialView.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class TrialView : Window
{
    public bool Proceed { get; private set; }

    public TrialView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        var vm = new TrialViewModel();
        vm.ActivationSucceeded += () => { Proceed = true; Close(); };
        DataContext = vm;
    }
}
</file>

</files>
